  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const boardEl=$('board'),rackEl=$('rack'),statusEl=$('status'),logEl=$('log');

  function makeBag(){ bag=[]; for(const [l,n] of Object.entries(DIST)) for(let i=0;i<n;i++) bag.push(l); shuffle(bag); }
  function shuffle(a){ for(let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[a[i],a[j]]=[a[j],a[i]];} return a; }
  function draw(n){ const out=[]; while(n-- > 0 && bag.length) out.push(bag.pop()); return out; }
  function newGame(skipReady=false){
    cancelTurnTransition();moveBusy=false;$('passOverlay').classList.remove('show');
    clearTimeout(moveResultHideTimer);clearTimeout(scoreBurstTimer);$('moveResult')?.classList.remove('show');$('scoreBurst')?.classList.remove('show');
    pauseClock();
    applyBattlefieldPreset(battlefieldPreset);board=Array.from({length:SIZE},()=>Array(SIZE).fill(null)); makeBag();
    players=[{hp:100,armor:0,rack:draw(7)},{hp:100,armor:0,rack:draw(7)}]; turn=gameMode==='cpu'?0:Math.floor(Math.random()*2); selectedRack.clear(); placements=[]; gameOver=false; cpuThinking=false; passStreak=0; battleControl=new Map(); siegeThreatPlayer=null; lastMoveCells=[];lastMovePlayer=null; reforgedThisTurn=false; lastAttackSummary=[null,null]; matchRecorded=false; matchStatSubmitted=false;if(gameMode==='online'&&onlineRole==='host')onlineMatchId=(crypto.randomUUID?.()||('match-'+Date.now()+'-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2))); matchStarted=false; stats={turns:0,totalDamage:0,biggestHit:0,swaps:0,combos:0,captures:0,counters:0,timeouts:0};
    const limit=TIMER_PRESETS[timerPreset]; clocks=[limit,limit]; clockRunning=false; clockLast=Date.now();
    $('p1Name').textContent=playerNames[0]; $('p2Name').textContent=playerNames[1];
    $('modeSelect').value=gameMode; $('beginTurnBtn').disabled=false;$('overlaySecondaryBtn').style.display='none'; $('difficultySelect').value=cpuDifficulty; $('difficultySelect').style.display=gameMode==='cpu'?'inline-block':'none'; $('timerSelect').value=timerPreset; $('battlefieldSelect').value=battlefieldPreset; $('beginTurnBtn').style.display='block';
    logEl.innerHTML=''; const starter=playerLabel(turn); setStatus(`${starter}: opening attack must cross the center medallion.`,'info'); renderAll(); updateConnectionBanner(); if(skipReady){matchStarted=true;startClock();if(gameMode==='online')syncOnlineState();}else showMatchReady();
  }

  function playerLabel(i){if(gameMode==='cpu')return i===0?playerNames[0]:'Computer';return playerNames[i]||`Player ${i+1}`;}
  function cleanName(v,fallback){v=String(v||'').replace(/[<>]/g,'').trim().slice(0,16);const toks=v.toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z]+/).filter(Boolean);if(toks.some(isContentBlocked))return fallback;return v||fallback;}
  function accountErrorText(code){
    if(code==='username_invalid')return 'Username must be 3–16 letters, numbers, or underscores.';
    if(code==='username_taken')return 'That username is already taken.';
    if(code==='password_invalid')return 'Password must be 8–72 characters.';
    if(code==='invalid_login')return 'Username or password is incorrect.';
    if(code==='password_required')return 'Password is required to delete the account.';
    if(code==='unauthorized')return 'Your sign-in expired. Sign in again.';
    return 'Account service could not complete that request.';
  }
  function renderAccountProfile(message='',kind=''){
    const box=$('accountBox');if(!box)return;const signed=!!accountProfile;box.classList.toggle('signedIn',signed);
    const msg=signed?$('accountSignedMessage'):$('accountMessage');if(msg){msg.textContent=message||'';msg.className='accountMessage'+(kind?' '+kind:'');}
    if(!signed)return;
    const s=accountProfile.stats||{},games=Number(s.games)||0,wins=Number(s.wins)||0;
    $('accountName').textContent=accountProfile.username||'Player';$('accountWins').textContent=wins;$('accountLosses').textContent=Number(s.losses)||0;$('accountGames').textContent=games;$('accountStreak').textContent=Number(s.streak)||0;$('accountBestStreak').textContent=Number(s.bestStreak)||0;$('accountWinRate').textContent=games?`${Math.round(wins/games*100)}%`:'0%';
    syncLaunchName(accountProfile.username);if($('onlineNameInput'))$('onlineNameInput').value=accountProfile.username;
  }
  async function accountRequest(path,{method='GET',body=null,auth=true}={}){
    const headers={'content-type':'application/json'};if(auth&&accountToken)headers.authorization=`Bearer ${accountToken}`;
    const r=await fetch(ACCOUNT_API+path,{method,headers,body:body==null?undefined:JSON.stringify(body)});let data={};try{data=await r.json()}catch(e){};if(!r.ok)throw Object.assign(new Error(data.error||'account_error'),{code:data.error||'account_error',status:r.status});return data;
  }
  async function refreshAccount(silent=false){
    if(!accountToken){accountProfile=null;renderAccountProfile();return false;}
    try{const d=await accountRequest('/auth/me');accountProfile=d.profile||null;renderAccountProfile(silent?'':'Stats refreshed.','ok');return !!accountProfile;}
    catch(e){if(e.status===401){accountToken='';accountProfile=null;storage.setItem('wordHavocAccountToken','');renderAccountProfile('Sign-in expired. Sign in again.','bad');}else renderAccountProfile('Connection unavailable. Your sign-in is saved; try again.','bad');return false;}
  }
  async function accountAuth(mode){
    if(accountBusy)return;const u=String($('accountUsername')?.value||'').trim(),p=String($('accountPassword')?.value||'');accountBusy=true;renderAccountProfile(mode==='register'?'Creating account…':'Signing in…');
    try{const d=await accountRequest(mode==='register'?'/auth/register':'/auth/login',{method:'POST',body:{username:u,password:p},auth:false});accountToken=d.token||'';accountProfile=d.profile||null;storage.setItem('wordHavocAccountToken',accountToken);if($('accountPassword'))$('accountPassword').value='';renderAccountProfile(mode==='register'?'Account created.':'Signed in.','ok');}
    catch(e){renderAccountProfile(accountErrorText(e.code),'bad');}finally{accountBusy=false;}
  }
  async function logoutAccount(){
    try{if(accountToken)await accountRequest('/auth/logout',{method:'POST'});}catch(e){}accountToken='';accountProfile=null;storage.setItem('wordHavocAccountToken','');renderAccountProfile('Signed out.','ok');
  }
  async function deleteAccount(){
    if(!accountProfile)return;const password=prompt(`Delete ${accountProfile.username}? Enter your password to permanently delete this account and its stats.`);if(password==null)return;if(!confirm('This permanently deletes the Word Havoc account and online stats. Continue?'))return;
    try{await accountRequest('/auth/delete',{method:'DELETE',body:{password}});accountToken='';accountProfile=null;storage.setItem('wordHavocAccountToken','');renderAccountProfile('Account deleted.','ok');}
    catch(e){renderAccountProfile(accountErrorText(e.code),'bad');}
  }
  async function submitOnlineAccountResult(result){
    if(matchStatSubmitted||gameMode!=='online'||onlineRole!=='host'||!accountToken||!onlineMatchId)return;matchStatSubmitted=true;
    try{await accountRequest('/stats/match',{method:'POST',body:{room:onlineCode,matchId:onlineMatchId,winner:result}});await refreshAccount(true);}
    catch(e){matchStatSubmitted=false;console.warn('Online stats could not be saved',e);}
  }
  function isLocalTurn(){if(remoteActionProcessing)return true;if(turnTransition||moveBusy||!matchStarted||(gameMode==='online'&&onlineRole==='guest'&&onlineActionPending))return false;if(gameMode==='online')return onlineConnected&&turn===onlinePlayerIndex;if(gameMode==='cpu')return turn===0;return true;}
  function toast(msg){const el=$('toast');if(!el)return;el.textContent=msg;el.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toast._t);toast._t=setTimeout(()=>el.classList.remove('show'),1800);}
  function pulsePlayerCard(i,kind='hit'){const el=$(`p${i+1}card`);if(!el||reducedMotion)return;const cls=kind==='heal'?'healPulse':'hitTaken';el.classList.remove(cls);void el.offsetWidth;el.classList.add(cls);setTimeout(()=>el.classList.remove(cls),430);}
  function initAudio(){if(!soundEnabled)return;try{audioCtx=audioCtx||new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();if(audioCtx.state==='suspended')audioCtx.resume();}catch(e){}}
  function sfx(kind){
    if(['attack','heal','armor','capture','win'].includes(kind))nativeHaptic(kind==='win'?'heavy':'medium');
    if(!soundEnabled||kind==='tick')return;
    initAudio();if(!audioCtx)return;
    const now=audioCtx.currentTime;
    const play=(freq,start=0,dur=.075,gain=.014,endFreq=null)=>{try{const o=audioCtx.createOscillator(),g=audioCtx.createGain();o.type='sine';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq,now+start);if(endFreq)o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(endFreq,now+start+dur);g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,now+start);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(gain,now+start+.008);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,now+start+dur);o.connect(g);g.connect(audioCtx.destination);o.start(now+start);o.stop(now+start+dur+.01);}catch(e){}};
    if(kind==='attack'){play(150,0,.085,.016,95);}
    else if(kind==='heal'){play(440,0,.08,.012,520);play(660,.07,.10,.010,720);}
    else if(kind==='armor'){play(210,0,.08,.012,250);play(315,.035,.09,.009,350);}
    else if(kind==='capture'){play(260,0,.07,.012,320);play(390,.055,.085,.009,430);}
    else if(kind==='win'){play(392,0,.11,.012,440);play(523,.09,.13,.011,587);play(659,.18,.16,.010,740);}
  }
  function updateConnectionBanner(msg,kind){const el=$('connectionBanner');if(!el)return;const sb=$('onlineSafetyBtn');if(sb)sb.style.display=(gameMode==='online'&&matchStarted)?'inline-block':'none';el.className='connectionBanner'+(kind?' '+kind:'');if(msg){el.textContent=msg;return;}if(gameMode==='online'){el.classList.add(onlineConnected?'online':'warn');el.textContent=onlineConnected?`ONLINE • ${onlineRole==='host'?'HOST':'GUEST'} • CODE ${onlineCode} • ${playerNames[0]} vs ${playerNames[1]}`:`ONLINE • CODE ${onlineCode||'------'} • CONNECTING…`;}else if(gameMode==='cpu'){el.textContent=`PRACTICE • ${cpuDifficulty.toUpperCase()} CPU • ${battlefieldPreset.toUpperCase()} • ${timerPreset.toUpperCase()}`;}else el.textContent=`LOCAL DUEL • ${battlefieldPreset.toUpperCase()} • ${timerPreset.toUpperCase()}`;}
  function clonePlayerForViewer(p,i,viewerIndex,includeSecrets=false){
    const copy={...p};
    copy.rack=(includeSecrets||i===viewerIndex)?[...p.rack]:Array(p.rack.length).fill('?');
    return copy;
  }
  function statePayload(viewerIndex,result=null,includeSecrets=false){
    const payload={type:'state',v:3,board,players:players.map((p,i)=>clonePlayerForViewer(p,i,viewerIndex,includeSecrets)),turn,battleControl:[...battleControl.entries()],passStreak,stats,gameOver,siegeThreatPlayer,lastMoveCells,lastMovePlayer,reforgedThisTurn,lastAttackSummary,clocks,matchStarted,timerPreset,battlefieldPreset,playerNames,onlineMatchId,logHtml:logEl.innerHTML,result};
    if(includeSecrets)payload.bag=[...bag]; else payload.bagCount=bag.length;
    return payload;
  }
  function syncOnlineState(result=null){
    if(gameMode!=='online'||onlineRole!=='host'||!onlineConn||!onlineConn.open)return;
    try{
      // The guest receives only Player 2's rack. Player 1's rack and the bag order never leave the host.
      onlineConn.send(statePayload(1,result,false));
      storage.setItem('wordHavocOnlineHostState',JSON.stringify({code:onlineCode,at:Date.now(),state:statePayload(0,result,true)}));
    }catch(e){console.warn(e);}
  }
  function applyRemoteState(m){
    if(!m||m.type!=='state'||onlineRole!=='guest')return;
    pauseClock();onlineActionPending=false;
    board=m.board||board;
    if(Number.isInteger(m.bagCount))bag=Array(Math.max(0,m.bagCount)).fill('?');
    players=m.players||players;
    turn=Number.isInteger(m.turn)?m.turn:turn;
    battleControl=new Map(m.battleControl||[]);passStreak=m.passStreak||0;stats=m.stats||stats;gameOver=!!m.gameOver;siegeThreatPlayer=m.siegeThreatPlayer??null;lastMoveCells=m.lastMoveCells||[];lastMovePlayer=m.lastMovePlayer??null;reforgedThisTurn=!!m.reforgedThisTurn;lastAttackSummary=Array.isArray(m.lastAttackSummary)?m.lastAttackSummary:[null,null];clocks=m.clocks||clocks;matchStarted=!!m.matchStarted;onlineMatchId=String(m.onlineMatchId||onlineMatchId);timerPreset=m.timerPreset||timerPreset;applyBattlefieldPreset(m.battlefieldPreset||battlefieldPreset);playerNames=(m.playerNames||playerNames).map((x,i)=>cleanName(x,`Player ${i+1}`));$('p1Name').textContent=playerNames[0];$('p2Name').textContent=playerNames[1];$('modeSelect').value='online';$('difficultySelect').style.display='none';$('timerSelect').value=timerPreset;$('battlefieldSelect').value=battlefieldPreset;if(typeof m.logHtml==='string'){const t=document.createElement('template');t.innerHTML=m.logHtml;for(const el of [...t.content.querySelectorAll('*')]){if(!['DIV','B','SPAN','BR'].includes(el.tagName)){el.replaceWith(document.createTextNode(el.textContent||''));continue;}for(const attr of [...el.attributes])el.removeAttribute(attr.name);if(el.tagName==='DIV')el.className='logItem';}logEl.replaceChildren(t.content);}placements=[];selectedRack.clear();$('passOverlay').classList.remove('show');$('beginTurnBtn').disabled=false;$('overlaySecondaryBtn').style.display='none';$('startOverlay').classList.remove('show');renderAll();updateConnectionBanner();if(m.result){if(m.result.draw)showStalemate(true);else showGameOver(m.result.winner,m.result.data||{},true);}else if(!gameOver){startClock();setStatus(isLocalTurn()?'Your turn — build your attack.':`${playerLabel(turn)} is thinking…`,'info');}
  }
  function sendGuestAction(action,data={}){
    if(gameMode!=='online'||onlineRole!=='guest'||!onlineConn||!onlineConn.open||onlineActionPending)return false;
    const id=`${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;
    onlineActionPending=true;
    try{onlineConn.send({type:'action',id,action,...data});setStatus('Move sent to host for validation…','info');renderRack();return true;}
    catch(e){onlineActionPending=false;setStatus('Could not send the move. Check the connection and try again.','bad');return false;}
  }
  async function processGuestAction(m){
    if(onlineRole!=='host'||gameOver||remoteActionProcessing||!m||m.type!=='action'||(turn!==1&&m.action!=='surrender'))return;
    if(m.id&&m.id===lastGuestActionId)return;lastGuestActionId=m.id||'';
    remoteActionProcessing=true;
    try{
      if(m.action==='move'){
        const incoming=Array.isArray(m.placements)?m.placements:[];
        if(!incoming.length||incoming.length>7){syncOnlineState();return;}
        const seenRack=new Set(),seenCell=new Set();const rebuilt=[];
        for(const q of incoming){
          const r=Number(q.r),c=Number(q.c),ri=Number(q.rackIndex),ck=`${r},${c}`;
          if(!Number.isInteger(r)||!Number.isInteger(c)||!Number.isInteger(ri)||r<0||r>=SIZE||c<0||c>=SIZE||ri<0||ri>=players[1].rack.length||seenRack.has(ri)||seenCell.has(ck)||board[r][c]){syncOnlineState();return;}
          seenRack.add(ri);seenCell.add(ck);rebuilt.push({r,c,rackIndex:ri,letter:players[1].rack[ri]});
        }
        placements=rebuilt;selectedRack.clear();
        await submitMove(false);
      }else if(m.action==='reforge'){
        const inds=(Array.isArray(m.indices)?m.indices:[]).map(Number);
        if(inds.some(x=>!Number.isInteger(x)||x<0||x>=players[1].rack.length)||new Set(inds).size!==inds.length){syncOnlineState();return;}
        selectedRack=new Set(inds);performSwap(m.mode);
      }else if(m.action==='pass'){
        placements=[];selectedRack.clear();passTurn();
      }else if(m.action==='surrender'){
        gameOver=true;showGameOver(0,{surrender:true});
      }
    }finally{remoteActionProcessing=false;}
  }
  function makeSocketConnection(ws){
    return {
      get open(){return ws.readyState===WebSocket.OPEN;},
      send(obj){if(ws.readyState===WebSocket.OPEN)ws.send(JSON.stringify(obj));},
      close(){try{ws.close(1000,'Leaving match');}catch(e){}}
    };
  }
  function relayErrorText(code){
    if(code==='HOST_NOT_FOUND')return 'Match not found. Check the code and make sure the host is still waiting.';
    if(code==='ROOM_IN_USE')return 'That match code is already in use. Create a new match.';
    if(code==='ROOM_FULL')return 'That match already has two players.';
    return 'Online connection failed. Try again.';
  }
  function scheduleRelayReconnect(){
    if(onlineClosing||gameMode!=='online'||!onlineRole||!onlineCode||gameOver)return;
    clearTimeout(relayRetryTimer);relayRetryTimer=setTimeout(()=>connectRelay(onlineRole,true),2200);
  }
  function connectRelay(role,isRetry=false){
    if(!onlineCode||!role)return;
    clearTimeout(relayRetryTimer);
    try{onlineSocket?.close();}catch(e){}
    const url=`${RELAY_WS}?room=${encodeURIComponent(onlineCode)}&role=${encodeURIComponent(role)}&player=${encodeURIComponent(localPlayerId)}`;
    let ws;
    try{ws=new WebSocket(url);}catch(e){$('launchStatus').textContent='Could not open the online connection.';scheduleRelayReconnect();return;}
    onlineSocket=ws;onlineConn=makeSocketConnection(ws);
    ws.addEventListener('open',()=>{
      if(ws!==onlineSocket)return;
      if(role==='host'){
        $('launchStatus').innerHTML=`Match code: <b class="bigCode">${onlineCode}</b><br>Send this code to your opponent. Waiting for them to join…`;
        $('shareInviteBtn').style.display='block';
      }else $('launchStatus').textContent=`Connected to relay. Joining ${onlineCode}…`;
      updateConnectionBanner(isRetry?'RECONNECTED TO MATCH RELAY':'ONLINE RELAY CONNECTED',isRetry?'online':'');if(onlineHeartbeatTimer)clearInterval(onlineHeartbeatTimer);onlineHeartbeatTimer=setInterval(()=>{if(onlineConn?.open)onlineConn.send({type:'heartbeat'});},30000);
    });
    ws.addEventListener('message',ev=>{
      if(ws!==onlineSocket)return;
      let m;try{m=JSON.parse(String(ev.data));}catch(e){return;}
      if(m?.type==='relayReady'){
        if(accountToken&&onlineConn?.open)onlineConn.send({type:'authenticate',token:accountToken});
        return;
      }
      if(m?.type==='heartbeatAck')return;
      if(m?.type==='authReady'){accountProfile=m.profile||accountProfile;renderAccountProfile();if(role==='guest'&&onlineConn?.open)onlineConn.send({type:'join',name:accountProfile?.username||playerNames[1],playerId:localPlayerId});return;}
      if(m?.type==='authError'){logoutAccount();$('launchStatus').textContent='Your sign-in expired. Sign in again.';return;}
      if(m?.type==='peerProfile'){const i=m.role==='host'?0:1;playerNames[i]=cleanName(m.username,playerNames[i]||`Player ${i+1}`);if($('p'+(i+1)+'Name'))$('p'+(i+1)+'Name').textContent=playerNames[i];return;}
      if(m?.type==='relayPeerConnected'){
        if(role==='host')$('launchStatus').textContent='Opponent connected. Verifying account…';
        return;
      }
      if(m?.type==='relayWaiting')return;
      if(m?.type==='relayPeerClosed'){
        onlineConnected=false;pauseClock();updateConnectionBanner('OPPONENT DISCONNECTED • match paused for reconnection','warn');setStatus('Opponent disconnected. The match is paused until they reconnect.','bad');return;
      }
      if(m?.type==='relayError'){
        onlineConnected=false;const msg=relayErrorText(m.code);$('launchStatus').textContent=msg;updateConnectionBanner('ONLINE CONNECTION ERROR','warn');return;
      }
      handleOnlineMessage(m);
    });
    ws.addEventListener('close',ev=>{
      if(ws!==onlineSocket||onlineClosing)return;
      if(onlineHeartbeatTimer){clearInterval(onlineHeartbeatTimer);onlineHeartbeatTimer=null;}onlineConnected=false;pauseClock();
      const hardError=[4001,4002,4004].includes(ev.code);
      if(!hardError){updateConnectionBanner('CONNECTION LOST • reconnecting…','warn');setStatus('Connection lost. The match is paused while reconnecting.','bad');scheduleRelayReconnect();}
    });
    ws.addEventListener('error',()=>{if(ws!==onlineSocket||onlineClosing)return;onlineConnected=false;updateConnectionBanner('ONLINE CONNECTION ERROR','warn');});
  }
  function handleOnlineMessage(m){
    if(!m||typeof m!=='object')return;
    if(m.type==='join'&&onlineRole==='host'){
      const guestId=String(m.playerId||'');if(guestId&&blockedPlayers.has(guestId)){onlineConn.send({type:'blocked'});return;}playerIds[1]=guestId;playerNames[1]=cleanName(m.name,'Opponent');onlineConnected=true;onlineConn.send({type:'welcome',hostName:playerNames[0],hostId:localPlayerId,code:onlineCode,timerPreset,battlefieldPreset});$('launchStatus').innerHTML=`<b>${playerNames[1]}</b> joined. Starting match…`;if(!matchStarted)newGame(true);else syncOnlineState();$('startOverlay').classList.remove('show');updateConnectionBanner();return;
    }
    if(m.type==='welcome'&&onlineRole==='guest'){
      const hostId=String(m.hostId||'');if(hostId&&blockedPlayers.has(hostId)){onlineConn.send({type:'blocked'});$('launchStatus').textContent='This player is blocked on this device.';try{onlineSocket?.close();}catch(e){}return;}playerIds[0]=hostId;playerNames[0]=cleanName(m.hostName,'Host');timerPreset=m.timerPreset||timerPreset;applyBattlefieldPreset(m.battlefieldPreset||battlefieldPreset);onlineCode=m.code||onlineCode;onlineConnected=true;updateConnectionBanner();return;
    }
    if(m.type==='blocked'){onlineConnected=false;pauseClock();$('launchStatus').textContent='This online connection was blocked.';$('startOverlay').classList.add('show');try{onlineSocket?.close();}catch(e){}return;}
    if(m.type==='state'){if(onlineRole==='guest')applyRemoteState(m);return;}
    if(m.type==='action'&&onlineRole==='host'){void processGuestAction(m);return;}
    if(m.type==='rematchRequest'){pendingIncomingRematch=true;waitingRematch=false;$('passOverlay').classList.add('show');$('overlayTitle').textContent='Rematch?';$('overlayText').textContent=`${playerLabel(1-onlinePlayerIndex)} wants another match.`;$('overlayDamage').style.display='none';$('beginTurnBtn').textContent='Accept Rematch';$('overlaySecondaryBtn').style.display='block';return;}
    if(m.type==='rematchAccept'&&onlineRole==='host'){pendingIncomingRematch=false;waitingRematch=false;newGame(true);syncOnlineState();return;}
    if(m.type==='surrender'&&onlineRole==='host'){gameOver=true;showGameOver(0,{surrender:true});return;}
  }
  function destroyOnline(){
    if($('shareInviteBtn'))$('shareInviteBtn').style.display='none';onlineClosing=true;clearTimeout(relayRetryTimer);relayRetryTimer=null;if(onlineHeartbeatTimer){clearInterval(onlineHeartbeatTimer);onlineHeartbeatTimer=null;}
    try{onlineSocket?.close(1000,'Leaving match');}catch(e){}
    onlineSocket=null;onlineConn=null;onlineConnected=false;onlineRole=null;onlineCode='';pendingIncomingRematch=false;waitingRematch=false;onlineActionPending=false;remoteActionProcessing=false;lastGuestActionId='';onlineClosing=false;
  }
  function randomCode(){const chars='ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';let x='';for(let i=0;i<6;i++)x+=chars[Math.floor(Math.random()*chars.length)];return x;}
  function createOnlineMatch(){
    if(!accountProfile||!accountToken){$('launchStatus').textContent='Sign in or create a Word Havoc account to play online.';return;}
    if(!$('onlineTermsCheck').checked){$('launchStatus').textContent='Agree to the Terms and fair-play rules before creating an online match.';return;}
    destroyOnline();gameMode='online';onlineRole='host';onlinePlayerIndex=0;onlineCode=randomCode();playerNames=[accountProfile.username,'Opponent'];playerIds=[localPlayerId,''];timerPreset=$('launchTimer').value;applyBattlefieldPreset($('launchMap').value);$('launchStatus').innerHTML=`Creating match <b>${onlineCode}</b>…`;connectRelay('host');
  }
  function joinOnlineMatch(){
    if(!accountProfile||!accountToken){$('launchStatus').textContent='Sign in or create a Word Havoc account to play online.';return;}
    if(!$('onlineTermsCheck').checked){$('launchStatus').textContent='Agree to the Terms and fair-play rules before joining an online match.';return;}
    const code=String($('joinCodeInput').value||'').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g,'').slice(0,6);if(code.length!==6){$('launchStatus').textContent='Enter the 6-character match code.';return;}
    destroyOnline();gameMode='online';onlineRole='guest';onlinePlayerIndex=1;onlineCode=code;playerNames=['Host',accountProfile.username];playerIds=['',localPlayerId];$('launchStatus').textContent=`Connecting to ${code}…`;connectRelay('guest');
  }
  async function reportOpponent(){
    if(gameMode!=='online'||!matchStarted){toast('No online opponent to report.');return;}
    const opponentIndex=1-onlinePlayerIndex,reportedId=playerIds[opponentIndex]||'';
    const body={room:onlineCode,reporterId:localPlayerId,reportedId,reason:$('reportReason').value||'other',version:'1.0.0'};
    try{const r=await fetch('https://word-combat-relay.up.railway.app/report',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});if(!r.ok)throw new Error('report failed');toast('Report submitted. Thank you.');$('safetyOverlay').classList.remove('show');}
    catch(e){toast('Report could not be sent. Try again when online.');}
  }
  function blockOpponent(){
    if(gameMode!=='online'||!matchStarted){$('safetyOverlay').classList.remove('show');return;}
    const id=playerIds[1-onlinePlayerIndex];if(id){blockedPlayers.add(id);storage.setItem('wordHavocBlockedPlayers',JSON.stringify([...blockedPlayers]));}
    try{onlineConn?.send({type:'blocked'});}catch(e){}
    $('safetyOverlay').classList.remove('show');destroyOnline();pauseClock();matchStarted=false;gameOver=true;$('startOverlay').classList.add('show');$('launchStatus').textContent='Opponent blocked on this device. Create or join a different match to play again.';updateConnectionBanner('MAIN MENU');
  }
  window.wordHavocBack=()=>{if($('settingsOverlay').classList.contains('show')){$('settingsOverlay').classList.remove('show');return true;}if($('helpOverlay').classList.contains('show')){$('helpOverlay').classList.remove('show');return true;}if($('passOverlay').classList.contains('show'))return true;if(matchStarted&&!gameOver){return false;}showMainMenu(true);return true;};
  function resetLaunchMenu(){const main=$('launchMainChoices');if(main)main.hidden=false;['launchOnlinePanel','launchCpuPanel','launchLocalPanel'].forEach(id=>{const el=$(id);if(el)el.hidden=true;});const opt=$('launchOptions');if(opt)opt.open=false;}
  function showMainMenu(force=false){if(matchStarted&&!gameOver&&!force&&!confirm('Leave the current match and return to the main menu?'))return;cancelTurnTransition();cpuThinking=false;matchStarted=false;pauseClock();if(gameMode==='online')destroyOnline();$('passOverlay').classList.remove('show');resetLaunchMenu();$('startOverlay').classList.add('show');$('launchStatus').textContent='';updateConnectionBanner('MAIN MENU');}
  function surrenderMatch(){if(!matchStarted||gameOver)return;if(!confirm('Surrender this match?'))return;if(gameMode==='online'&&onlineRole==='guest'){if(onlineConn?.open){onlineConn.send({type:'action',id:`${Date.now()}-surrender`,action:'surrender'});setStatus('Surrender sent. Waiting for host confirmation…','info');}return;}const loser=gameMode==='online'?onlinePlayerIndex:turn;gameOver=true;showGameOver(1-loser,{surrender:true});}
  function formatClock(ms){if(ms==null)return '∞';const sec=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(ms/1000)),m=Math.floor(sec/60),s=sec%60;return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2,'0')}`;}
  function syncClock(){
    if(!clockRunning||gameOver||TIMER_PRESETS[timerPreset]==null)return;
    const now=Date.now(),elapsed=Math.max(0,now-clockLast);clockLast=now;clocks[turn]=Math.max(0,clocks[turn]-elapsed);
    if(clocks[turn]<=0){clockRunning=false;if(gameMode!=='online'||onlineRole==='host')handleTimeout(turn);}
  }
  function startClock(){if(gameOver||!matchStarted||TIMER_PRESETS[timerPreset]==null){renderClocks();return;}clockLast=Date.now();clockRunning=true;renderClocks();}
  function pauseClock(){if(clockRunning)syncClock();clockRunning=false;renderClocks();}
  function renderClocks(){
    const limit=TIMER_PRESETS[timerPreset];
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
      const el=$('clock'+(i+1));if(!el)continue;const shown=(i===turn?clocks[i]:limit);el.textContent=formatClock(shown);el.classList.toggle('activeClock',clockRunning&&turn===i&&!gameOver);
      el.classList.toggle('clockLow',shown!=null&&shown<=20000&&shown>10000);el.classList.toggle('clockCritical',shown!=null&&shown<=10000&&!gameOver&&i===turn);
    }
    const active=$('activeClock'); if(active){const ms=clocks[turn];active.textContent=formatClock(ms);active.classList.toggle('low',ms!=null&&ms<=20000&&ms>10000);active.classList.toggle('critical',ms!=null&&ms<=10000&&!gameOver);}
  }
  function handleTimeout(who){
    if(gameOver)return;clockRunning=false;clocks[who]=0;placements=[];selectedRack.clear();$('swapPanel').classList.remove('show');lastMovePlayer=who;lastMoveCells=[];
    const burn=Math.min(TIMEOUT_HP_COST,Math.max(0,players[who].hp));players[who].hp=Math.max(0,players[who].hp-TIMEOUT_HP_COST);passStreak++;stats.turns++;stats.timeouts++;
    addLog(`<b>${playerLabel(who)}</b> ran out of turn time, forfeited the move, and burned <b>${burn} HP</b>. <span style="color:#8f9aaa">(${passStreak}/4 stall passes)</span>`);renderAll();
    if(players[who].hp<=0){gameOver=true;showGameOver(1-who,{timeoutPenalty:true});return;}
    if(passStreak>=4){gameOver=true;const p1=players[0].hp,p2=players[1].hp;if(p1===p2)showStalemate();else showGameOver(p1>p2?0:1,{word:'',damage:0,stalemate:true});return;}
    showPass(who,0,`${playerLabel(who)} ran out of time, lost ${burn} HP, and forfeited the turn.`,false);
  }
  function controlCounts(){const c=[0,0];for(const owner of battleControl.values())if(owner===0||owner===1)c[owner]++;return c;}
  function qualifiesSiege(who){const c=controlCounts();return c[who]>=SIEGE_TARGET&&(c[who]-c[1-who])>=SIEGE_MARGIN;}
  function refreshSiegeThreat(attacker){
    if(siegeThreatPlayer!=null&&!qualifiesSiege(siegeThreatPlayer)){addLog(`<b>${playerLabel(siegeThreatPlayer)}</b>'s Siege threat was broken.`);siegeThreatPlayer=null;}
    if(qualifiesSiege(attacker)&&siegeThreatPlayer!==attacker){siegeThreatPlayer=attacker;const c=controlCounts();addLog(`<b>SIEGE THREAT:</b> ${playerLabel(attacker)} controls ${c[attacker]} battle squares and leads by ${c[attacker]-c[1-attacker]}. Break it before their next turn.`);}
    renderSiege();
  }
  function checkSiegeAtTurnStart(){
    if(siegeThreatPlayer!=null&&!qualifiesSiege(siegeThreatPlayer))siegeThreatPlayer=null;
    if(siegeThreatPlayer===turn&&qualifiesSiege(turn)){gameOver=true;pauseClock();renderAll();showGameOver(turn,{siege:true});return true;}
    renderSiege();return false;
  }
  function renderSiege(){
    const c=controlCounts();for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const el=$('siege'+(i+1));if(el){el.textContent=`CONTROL ${c[i]}/${SIEGE_TARGET}`;el.classList.toggle('threat',siegeThreatPlayer===i);}}
    const a=$('siegeAlert');if(!a)return;
    if(siegeThreatPlayer==null){a.classList.remove('show');a.textContent='';return;}
    const p=siegeThreatPlayer,lead=c[p]-c[1-p];a.innerHTML=`SIEGE ALERT — <b>${playerLabel(p)}</b> controls ${c[p]} battle squares and leads by ${lead}. ${playerLabel(1-p)} must break the ${SIEGE_TARGET}+ / +${SIEGE_MARGIN} condition this turn or lose.`;a.classList.add('show');
  }
  function showMatchReady(){
    if(gameMode==='online')return;pauseClock();$('overlaySecondaryBtn').style.display='none';$('overlayTitle').textContent='Match Ready';$('overlayDamage').style.display='none';
    const timeText=TIMER_PRESETS[timerPreset]==null?'Untimed practice':`${formatClock(TIMER_PRESETS[timerPreset])} turn timer • timeout = -${TIMEOUT_HP_COST} HP + forfeited turn`;
    const diffText=gameMode==='cpu'?`<br><b>Computer difficulty: ${cpuDifficulty.toUpperCase()}</b>`:'';
    const starterVerb=playerLabel(turn)==='You'?'start':'starts';$('overlayText').innerHTML=`<b>${playerLabel(turn)}</b> ${starterVerb}. Your attack total is the damage dealt before Armor. No hidden opening reduction.<br><br><b>${timeText}</b>${diffText}<br>Win by knockout or by holding a Siege control advantage.`;
    $('beginTurnBtn').style.display='block';$('beginTurnBtn').textContent=`Begin ${playerLabel(turn)} Turn`;$('passOverlay').classList.add('show');
  }

  function renderBoard(){
    boardEl.innerHTML='';
    for(let r=0;r<SIZE;r++) for(let c=0;c<SIZE;c++){
      const key=`${r},${c}`;
      const isSpecial=HEAL_SQUARES.has(key)||ATTACK_SQUARES.has(key)||FORTIFY_SQUARES.has(key)||CONTROL_SQUARES.has(key)||CRIT_SQUARES.has(key);
      const isCenter=(r===CENTER&&c===CENTER);
      const isCenterStart=isCenter&&!isSpecial;
      const cell=document.createElement('div'); cell.className='cell'+(isCenterStart?' bonusStart':''); if(isCenterStart&&!board.some(row=>row.some(Boolean))&&!placements.length)cell.classList.add('centerGuide'); cell.dataset.r=r;cell.dataset.c=c;cell.tabIndex=0;cell.setAttribute('role','button');
      if(HEAL_SQUARES.has(key))cell.classList.add('bonusHeal'); if(ATTACK_SQUARES.has(key))cell.classList.add('bonusAttack'); if(FORTIFY_SQUARES.has(key))cell.classList.add('bonusFortify'); if(CONTROL_SQUARES.has(key))cell.classList.add('bonusControl'); if(CRIT_SQUARES.has(key))cell.classList.add('bonusCrit');
      const committed=board[r][c]; const pending=placements.find(p=>p.r===r&&p.c===c);
      const controller=battleControl.get(key); if(committed&&controller===0){cell.classList.add('controlP1');cell.title='Player 1 controls this battle square';} if(committed&&controller===1){cell.classList.add('controlP2');cell.title='Player 2 controls this battle square';}
      if(committed&&lastMovePlayer===1-turn&&lastMoveCells.includes(key))cell.classList.add('lastMoveMark');
      if(committed||pending){ const shown=pending||committed; cell.classList.add('occupied'); cell.classList.toggle('pending',!!pending); const owner=(pending?turn:shown.owner); if(owner===0)cell.classList.add('wordP1'); if(owner===1)cell.classList.add('wordP2'); cell.innerHTML=tileHTML(shown.letter,!!pending); }
      const bonusLabel=HEAL_SQUARES.has(key)?'heal square':ATTACK_SQUARES.has(key)?'attack square':FORTIFY_SQUARES.has(key)?'armor square':CONTROL_SQUARES.has(key)?'control square':CRIT_SQUARES.has(key)?'critical square':isCenter?'start square':'board square';cell.setAttribute('aria-label',`${String.fromCharCode(65+c)}${r+1}, ${bonusLabel}${committed?`, letter ${committed.letter}`:''}`);cell.addEventListener('click',()=>cellClick(r,c));cell.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();cellClick(r,c);}}); boardEl.appendChild(cell);
    }
  }
  function tileHTML(letter,pending){letter=/^[A-Z]$/.test(String(letter))?String(letter):'?';return `<div class="tile ${pending?'pendingTile':''}">${letter}<small>${VALUES[letter]}</small></div>`}
  function isCpuTurn(){return gameMode==='cpu'&&turn===1;}
  function rackOwnerIndex(){
    if(gameMode==='cpu')return 0;
    if(gameMode==='online')return onlinePlayerIndex;
    return turn;
  }
  function renderRack(){
    rackEl.innerHTML='';
    const owner=rackOwnerIndex(),rack=players[owner]?.rack||[];
    const canAct=!gameOver&&!cpuThinking&&turn===owner&&isLocalTurn()&&!isCpuTurn();
    rack.forEach((l,i)=>{
      const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='rackTile';b.dataset.i=i;
      const actualLetter=/^[A-Z]$/.test(String(l))?String(l):'?';
      b.innerHTML=actualLetter==='?'?'?':`${actualLetter}<small>${VALUES[actualLetter]}</small>`;
      const used=owner===turn&&placements.some(p=>p.rackIndex===i); if(used)b.classList.add('used');
      if(owner===turn&&selectedRack.has(i))b.classList.add('selected');
      if(!canAct)b.classList.add('rackWaiting');
      b.disabled=!canAct;b.addEventListener('click',()=>rackClick(i));rackEl.appendChild(b);
    });
    if(gameMode==='cpu') $('rackTitle').textContent=turn===0?'Your Rack':'Your Rack — computer turn';
    else if(gameMode==='online') $('rackTitle').textContent=turn===onlinePlayerIndex?'Your Rack':'Your Rack — opponent turn';
    else $('rackTitle').textContent=`${playerLabel(turn)} Rack`;
    $('turnLabel').textContent=isCpuTurn()?(cpuThinking?'CPU THINKING':'CPU TURN'):(gameMode==='online'?(isLocalTurn()?'YOUR TURN':`${playerLabel(turn)} TURN`):(gameMode==='cpu'?'YOUR TURN':`P${turn+1} TURN`)); $('turnLabel').classList.toggle('cpuThinking',cpuThinking);
    $('bagCount').textContent=`Bag: ${bag.length}`;
    const lock=isCpuTurn()||cpuThinking||gameOver||!isLocalTurn();
    ['recallBtn','swapBtn','passBtn','submitBtn'].forEach(id=>$(id).disabled=lock);
  }
  function renderLastAttack(){
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
      const el=$('lastAttack'+(i+1)); if(!el)continue; const m=lastAttackSummary[i];
      const score=el.querySelector('b'), word=el.querySelector('em');
      if(!m){score.textContent='—';word.textContent='NO ATTACK YET';el.classList.remove('scored');continue;}
      score.textContent=`+${m.damage}`;word.textContent=`${String(m.word||'MOVE').toUpperCase()}  •  ${m.damage} POINTS`;el.classList.add('scored');
    }
  }
  function renderHP(){
    players.forEach((p,i)=>{$('hp'+(i+1)).textContent=Math.max(0,p.hp);$('hpbar'+(i+1)).style.width=(Math.max(0,Math.min(MAX_HP,p.hp))/MAX_HP*100)+'%';const a=$('armor'+(i+1));if(a){const armor=p.armor||0;a.textContent=`ARMOR ${armor}/${MAX_ARMOR}`;a.style.setProperty('--armorFill',`${(armor/MAX_ARMOR)*100}%`);a.classList.toggle('armed',armor>0);} });
    $('p1card').classList.toggle('active',turn===0&&!gameOver);$('p2card').classList.toggle('active',turn===1&&!gameOver);
    $('p1card').classList.toggle('lowHP',players[0].hp<=25);$('p2card').classList.toggle('lowHP',players[1].hp<=25); renderLastAttack(); renderClocks(); renderSiege();
  }
  function getMatchHistory(){try{return JSON.parse(storage.getItem('wordHavocMatchHistory')||'[]')}catch(e){return []}}
  function recordMatch(winner){
    if(matchRecorded)return; matchRecorded=true; const history=getMatchHistory();
    history.push({v:'1.0.0',mode:gameMode,difficulty:cpuDifficulty,timer:timerPreset,turns:stats.turns,totalDamage:stats.totalDamage,biggestHit:stats.biggestHit,swaps:stats.swaps,combos:stats.combos,captures:stats.captures,counters:stats.counters,timeouts:stats.timeouts,winner,p1hp:Math.max(0,players[0].hp),p2hp:Math.max(0,players[1].hp),p1armor:players[0].armor||0,p2armor:players[1].armor||0,control:controlCounts(),at:Date.now()});
    storage.setItem('wordHavocMatchHistory',JSON.stringify(history.slice(-20)));
  }
  function renderStats(){
    $('statTurns').textContent=stats.turns; $('statDamage').textContent=stats.totalDamage; $('statBiggest').textContent=stats.biggestHit; $('statSwaps').textContent=stats.swaps; $('statCombos').textContent=stats.combos; $('statCaptures').textContent=stats.captures; $('statCounters').textContent=stats.counters; $('statTimeouts').textContent=stats.timeouts;
    const h=getMatchHistory(); if(h.length){const sample=h.slice(-10),avg=sample.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.turns,0)/sample.length,hit=sample.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.biggestHit,0)/sample.length;$('balanceLine').textContent=`Local balance data: ${h.length} completed match${h.length===1?'':'es'} • recent avg ${avg.toFixed(1)} turns • avg biggest hit ${hit.toFixed(1)}.`;} else $('balanceLine').textContent='Complete matches to build balance data on this device.';
  }
  function renderAll(){renderBoard();renderRack();renderHP();renderStats();updatePreview();renderClocks();renderSiege();}

  function rackClick(i){
    if(gameOver||isCpuTurn()||cpuThinking||!isLocalTurn())return;
    if(placements.some(p=>p.rackIndex===i)) return;
    const swapping=$('swapPanel').classList.contains('show');
    if(swapping){
      if(selectedRack.has(i)) selectedRack.delete(i); else selectedRack.add(i);
    } else {
      if(selectedRack.has(i)) selectedRack.clear(); else { selectedRack.clear(); selectedRack.add(i); }
    }
    renderRack(); updateSwapButtons();
  }
  function cellClick(r,c){
    if(gameOver||isCpuTurn()||cpuThinking||!isLocalTurn())return;
    const existing=board[r][c]; const pi=placements.findIndex(p=>p.r===r&&p.c===c);
    if(pi>=0){ selectedRack.add(placements[pi].rackIndex); placements.splice(pi,1); renderAll(); return; }
    if(existing)return;
    let idx=[...selectedRack][0];
    if(idx==null){ idx=players[turn].rack.findIndex((_,i)=>!placements.some(p=>p.rackIndex===i)); if(idx<0)return; }
    selectedRack.delete(idx); placements.push({r,c,letter:players[turn].rack[idx],rackIndex:idx}); renderAll();
  }
  function recall(){ if(isCpuTurn()||cpuThinking||!isLocalTurn())return; placements=[]; selectedRack.clear(); renderAll(); setStatus('Tiles recalled.','info'); }

  function getMainWord(){
    if(!placements.length)return null;
    const sameRow=placements.every(p=>p.r===placements[0].r), sameCol=placements.every(p=>p.c===placements[0].c);
    if(!sameRow&&!sameCol)return {error:'All new tiles must be in one row or one column.'};
    let dir;
    if(placements.length===1){
      const p=placements[0]; const horiz=(c)=>c>=0&&c<SIZE&&(board[p.r][c]||placements.find(x=>x.r===p.r&&x.c===c));
      const vert=(r)=>r>=0&&r<SIZE&&(board[r][p.c]||placements.find(x=>x.r===r&&x.c===p.c));
      dir=(horiz(p.c-1)||horiz(p.c+1))?'H':(vert(p.r-1)||vert(p.r+1))?'V':'H';
    } else dir=sameRow?'H':'V';
    const fixed=(r,c)=>placements.find(p=>p.r===r&&p.c===c)?.letter||board[r][c]?.letter||null;
    let r=placements[0].r,c=placements[0].c;
    if(dir==='H'){while(c>0&&fixed(r,c-1))c--;}else{while(r>0&&fixed(r-1,c))r--;}
    let word='',cells=[];
    while(r<SIZE&&c<SIZE&&fixed(r,c)){word+=fixed(r,c);cells.push({r,c}); if(dir==='H')c++;else r++;}
    return {word,dir,cells};
  }

  function analyzeMove(){
    if(!placements.length)return {ok:false,msg:'Place at least one tile.'};
    const main=getMainWord(); if(main.error)return {ok:false,msg:main.error};
    if(main.word.length<2)return {ok:false,msg:'Words must contain at least 2 letters.'};
    if(!placements.every(p=>main.cells.some(x=>x.r===p.r&&x.c===p.c)))return {ok:false,msg:'Your tiles must form one continuous word.'};
    const hasBoard=board.some(row=>row.some(Boolean));
    if(!hasBoard){ if(!main.cells.some(x=>x.r===CENTER&&x.c===CENTER))return {ok:false,msg:'The opening word must cover the gold center medallion.'}; }
    else {
      let connected=false;
      for(const p of placements){
        if((p.r>0&&board[p.r-1][p.c])||(p.r<SIZE-1&&board[p.r+1][p.c])||(p.c>0&&board[p.r][p.c-1])||(p.c<SIZE-1&&board[p.r][p.c+1])) connected=true;
      }
      if(main.cells.some(x=>board[x.r][x.c]))connected=true;
      if(!connected)return {ok:false,msg:'Your word must connect to the existing board.'};
    }
    const fixed=(r,c)=>placements.find(p=>p.r===r&&p.c===c)?.letter||board[r]?.[c]?.letter||null;
    function collectWord(r,c,d){
      if(d==='H'){while(c>0&&fixed(r,c-1))c--;}else{while(r>0&&fixed(r-1,c))r--;}
      let word='',cells=[];
      while(r<SIZE&&c<SIZE&&fixed(r,c)){word+=fixed(r,c);cells.push({r,c});if(d==='H')c++;else r++;}
      return {word,cells,dir:d};
    }
    const crossEntries=[];
    for(const p of placements){
      const entry=collectWord(p.r,p.c,main.dir==='H'?'V':'H');
      if(entry.word.length>1)crossEntries.push(entry);
    }
    const allEntries=[main,...crossEntries];
    const allWords=allEntries.map(x=>x.word);
    const uniqueCells=new Map();
    for(const entry of allEntries)for(const cell of entry.cells)uniqueCells.set(`${cell.r},${cell.c}`,cell);
    const battleCells=[...uniqueCells.keys()].filter(k=>HEAL_SQUARES.has(k)||ATTACK_SQUARES.has(k)||FORTIFY_SQUARES.has(k)||CONTROL_SQUARES.has(k)||CRIT_SQUARES.has(k));
    const ownedUsed=battleCells.filter(k=>battleControl.get(k)===turn).length;
    const captureCells=battleCells.filter(k=>battleControl.has(k)&&battleControl.get(k)!==turn);
    const claimCells=battleCells.filter(k=>!battleControl.has(k));
    const base=[...main.word].reduce((s,l)=>s+(VALUES[l]||0),0);
    const bonus=LENGTH_BONUS[main.word.length]??(23+(main.word.length-11)*5);
    const healCount=placements.filter(p=>HEAL_SQUARES.has(`${p.r},${p.c}`)).length;
    const attackCount=placements.filter(p=>ATTACK_SQUARES.has(`${p.r},${p.c}`)).length;
    const fortifyCount=placements.filter(p=>FORTIFY_SQUARES.has(`${p.r},${p.c}`)).length;
    const controlTileCount=placements.filter(p=>CONTROL_SQUARES.has(`${p.r},${p.c}`)).length;
    const critCount=placements.filter(p=>CRIT_SQUARES.has(`${p.r},${p.c}`)).length;
    const heal=healCount*5, fortify=fortifyCount*FORTIFY_GAIN;
    const multiplier=attackCount>0?2:1;
    const rawDamage=base+bonus;
    const comboBonus=Math.min(6,crossEntries.length*3);
    const controlBonus=Math.min(6,ownedUsed*2)+(controlTileCount*2);
    const critBonus=critCount*6;
    const breachBonus=Math.min(4,captureCells.length*2);
    const counterCells=lastMovePlayer===1-turn?[...uniqueCells.keys()].filter(k=>lastMoveCells.includes(k)):[];
    const counterBonus=counterCells.length>=2?4:(counterCells.length===1?2:0);
    // The red square doubles only the core word attack. Tactical bonuses are added after the multiplier.
    const damage=(base*multiplier)+bonus+comboBonus+controlBonus+critBonus+breachBonus+counterBonus;
    return {ok:true,word:main.word,allWords,allEntries,crossEntries,crossCount:crossEntries.length,damage,rawDamage,base,bonus,comboBonus,controlBonus,critBonus,breachBonus,counterBonus,counterCount:counterCells.length,counterCells,heal,healCount,fortify,fortifyCount,attackCount,controlTileCount,critCount,multiplier,main,battleCells,captureCells,claimCells,captureCount:captureCells.length,claimCount:claimCells.length,ownedUsed};
  }

  const CONTENT_BLOCKLIST=new Set(`arsed\narsing\nasshole\nassholes\nblowjob\nblowjobs\nbullshit\nbullshits\nbullshitted\nbullshitter\nbullshitters\nbullshitting\nchink\nchinks\ncocksucker\ncocksuckers\ncrap\ncrapped\ncrapper\ncrappers\ncrapping\ncrappy\ncraps\ncumming\ncums\ncunt\ncunts\ndickhead\ndicks\nfag\nfaggot\nfaggots\nfags\nfart\nfarted\nfarting\nfarts\nfuck\nfucked\nfucking\nfucks\ngook\ngooks\nkike\nkikes\nkillself\nkys\nmotherfucker\nmotherfuckers\nmotherfucking\nnazi\nnazis\nnigga\nniggas\nnigger\nniggers\npiss\npissed\npisser\npissers\npisses\npissing\nrape\nraped\nrapes\nraping\nrapist\nrapists\nretard\nretarded\nretards\nshit\nshits\nshitted\nshitting\nshitty\nslut\nsluts\nspic\nspics\nturd\nturds\ntwat\ntwats\nwank\nwanked\nwanker\nwankers\nwanking\nwanks\nwetback\nwetbacks\nwhore\nwhores`.split('\n'));
  function isContentBlocked(word){return CONTENT_BLOCKLIST.has(String(word||'').toLowerCase());}

  const rememberedWords=new Set(JSON.parse(storage.getItem('wordHavocRememberedWords')||'[]'));
  let pendingUnknownWords=[];
  function rememberWords(words){
    words.forEach(w=>rememberedWords.add(String(w).toLowerCase()));
    storage.setItem('wordHavocRememberedWords',JSON.stringify([...rememberedWords]));
  }
  async function validateWord(word){
    const w=String(word).trim().toLowerCase();
    if(isContentBlocked(w))return false;
    // v1.0 uses only the bundled lexicon for deterministic, offline-safe validation.
    // Local/CPU play may remember a player-approved rare word; online play never allows manual overrides.
    return ENGLISH_WORDS.has(w)||rememberedWords.has(w);
  }

  let moveResultHideTimer=null,scoreBurstTimer=null;
  function showMoveResult(who,a,appliedDamage,hpDamage,absorbed){
    const el=$('moveResult'); if(!el||!a)return;
    clearTimeout(moveResultHideTimer);clearTimeout(scoreBurstTimer);
    const tactical=(a.comboBonus||0)+(a.controlBonus||0)+(a.breachBonus||0)+(a.counterBonus||0);
    const wordValue=(a.base||0)+(a.bonus||0);
    const scorer=gameMode==='cpu'?(who===0?'YOU':'COMPUTER'):(gameMode==='online'&&who===onlinePlayerIndex?'YOU':playerLabel(who));
    $('moveResultWho').textContent=`${scorer.toUpperCase()} · WORD SCORE`;
    $('moveResultWord').textContent=String(a.word||'MOVE').toUpperCase();
    $('moveResultDamage').textContent=`+${appliedDamage} SCORE`;
    let detail=`Word value ${wordValue}`;
    if(a.multiplier===2) detail+=` • ×2 letter hit`;
    if(tactical) detail+=` • +${tactical} tactics`;
    if(absorbed) detail+=` • ${absorbed} blocked → ${hpDamage} HP`;
    $('moveResultMath').textContent=detail;
    el.className='moveResultBar show '+(who===0?'playerResult':'cpuResult');
    const burst=$('scoreBurst');if(burst){$('scoreBurstWho').textContent=`${scorer.toUpperCase()} SCORED`;$('scoreBurstValue').textContent=`+${appliedDamage}`;$('scoreBurstWord').textContent=String(a.word||'MOVE').toUpperCase();burst.className='scoreBurst '+(who===0?'playerBurst':'cpuBurst');void burst.offsetWidth;burst.classList.add('show');scoreBurstTimer=setTimeout(()=>burst.classList.remove('show'),3000);}
    moveResultHideTimer=setTimeout(()=>{el.classList.remove('show');},7200);
  }

  async function submitMove(forceAccept=false){
    forceAccept=forceAccept===true&&gameMode!=='online';
    const automatedCpuMove=gameMode==='cpu'&&turn===1;
    if(gameOver||moveBusy||turnTransition||!matchStarted||(!automatedCpuMove&&!remoteActionProcessing&&!isLocalTurn())||(cpuThinking&&turn===0))return; const a=analyzeMove(); if(!a.ok){setStatus(a.msg,'bad');return;}if(a.allWords.some(isContentBlocked)){setStatus('That word is not permitted in Word Havoc matches.','bad');return;}
    moveBusy=true;const submissionEpoch=matchEpoch;
    try{
    const resumeAfterInvalid=clockRunning; pauseClock(); $('submitBtn').disabled=true; $('acceptWordBtn').style.display='none';
    setStatus(`Checking ${a.allWords.join(', ')}…`,'info');
    const unknown=[];
    if(!forceAccept){
      for(const w of a.allWords){ if(!(await validateWord(w))) unknown.push(w); }
      if(submissionEpoch!==matchEpoch)return;
      if(unknown.length){
        pendingUnknownWords=unknown;
        $('submitBtn').disabled=false;
        if(gameMode==='online'){
          $('acceptWordBtn').style.display='none';
          setStatus(`${unknown.join(', ')} is not in the competitive dictionary and could not be confirmed. Online matches do not allow manual word overrides.`,`bad`);
        }else{
          $('acceptWordBtn').style.display='block';
          $('acceptWordBtn').textContent=`Accept & Remember ${unknown.join(', ')}`;
          setStatus(`${unknown.join(', ')} could not be confirmed. If it is a legitimate English word, use Accept & Remember.`,`bad`);
        }
        moveBusy=false;renderRack();if(resumeAfterInvalid)startClock(); return;
      }
    } else if(pendingUnknownWords.length){ rememberWords(pendingUnknownWords); }
    pendingUnknownWords=[]; $('acceptWordBtn').style.display='none';
    if(gameMode==='online'&&onlineRole==='guest'&&!remoteActionProcessing){
      $('submitBtn').disabled=false;
      const sent=sendGuestAction('move',{placements:placements.map(p=>({r:p.r,c:p.c,rackIndex:p.rackIndex}))});
      if(resumeAfterInvalid&&sent)startClock();
      return;
    }
    moveBusy=false;
    const isOpening=!board.some(row=>row.some(Boolean));
    for(const p of placements)board[p.r][p.c]={letter:p.letter,owner:turn};
    const used=[...placements].sort((a,b)=>b.rackIndex-a.rackIndex).map(p=>p.rackIndex);
    used.forEach(i=>players[turn].rack.splice(i,1)); players[turn].rack.push(...draw(7-players[turn].rack.length)); renderRack();
    const attacker=turn,defender=1-turn;
    const appliedDamage=a.damage;
    const absorbed=Math.min(players[defender].armor||0,appliedDamage);players[defender].armor=Math.max(0,(players[defender].armor||0)-absorbed);const hpDamage=appliedDamage-absorbed;players[defender].hp-=hpDamage;
    passStreak=0; stats.turns++; stats.totalDamage+=appliedDamage; stats.biggestHit=Math.max(stats.biggestHit,appliedDamage); if(a.crossCount>0)stats.combos++; stats.captures+=a.captureCount;if(a.counterBonus>0)stats.counters++; renderStats();
    lastMoveCells=isOpening?[]:placements.map(p=>`${p.r},${p.c}`);lastMovePlayer=attacker;
    for(const key of a.battleCells)battleControl.set(key,attacker); refreshSiegeThreat(attacker);
    const beforeHeal=players[attacker].hp;
    if(a.heal>0)players[attacker].hp=Math.min(MAX_HP,players[attacker].hp+a.heal);
    pulsePlayerCard(defender,'hit');
    const healed=players[attacker].hp-beforeHeal;const beforeArmor=players[attacker].armor||0;if(a.fortify>0)players[attacker].armor=Math.min(MAX_ARMOR,beforeArmor+a.fortify);const fortified=players[attacker].armor-beforeArmor;if(healed>0)pulsePlayerCard(attacker,'heal');
    let effectText='';
    if(a.multiplier===2)effectText+=' <b>×2 HIT</b> activated.';
    if(a.comboBonus>0)effectText+=` <b>Crossfire +${a.comboBonus}</b>.`;
    if(a.controlBonus>0)effectText+=` <b>Control pressure +${a.controlBonus}</b>.`;
    if(a.breachBonus>0)effectText+=` <b>Breach +${a.breachBonus}</b>.`;
    if(a.counterBonus>0)effectText+=` <b>Counterstrike +${a.counterBonus}</b>.`;
    if(a.captureCount>0)effectText+=` Captured <b>${a.captureCount}</b> battle square${a.captureCount>1?'s':''}.`;
    if(a.claimCount>0)effectText+=` Claimed <b>${a.claimCount}</b> battle square${a.claimCount>1?'s':''}.`;
    if(healed>0)effectText+=` Restored <b>${healed} HP</b>.`;if(fortified>0)effectText+=` Gained <b>${fortified} Armor</b>.`;if(absorbed>0)effectText+=` Opponent armor absorbed <b>${absorbed}</b> damage.`;
    lastAttackSummary[attacker]={word:a.word,damage:appliedDamage,hpDamage,absorbed}; renderLastAttack();
    const scorePlate=$('lastAttack'+(attacker+1));if(scorePlate&&!reducedMotion){scorePlate.classList.remove('freshScore');void scorePlate.offsetWidth;scorePlate.classList.add('freshScore');setTimeout(()=>scorePlate.classList.remove('freshScore'),700);}
    const attackerLabel=playerLabel(attacker);if(a.heal>0)sfx('heal');if(a.fortify>0)sfx('armor');if(a.captureCount>0)sfx('capture');else sfx('attack');
    addLog(`<b>${attackerLabel}</b> played <b>${a.word}</b> for <b>${appliedDamage}</b> attack damage${absorbed?` (${absorbed} blocked, ${hpDamage} HP lost)`:''}.${effectText}`);
    placements=[]; selectedRack.clear(); $('submitBtn').disabled=false;
    if(players[defender].hp<=0){players[defender].hp=0;gameOver=true;renderAll();showGameOver(attacker,{...a,damage:appliedDamage});return;}
    renderHP();
    const overlayEffects=(a.multiplier===2?' <b>×2 HIT</b> activated.':'')+(a.comboBonus?` <b>Crossfire +${a.comboBonus}.</b>`:'')+(a.controlBonus?` <b>Control +${a.controlBonus}.</b>`:'')+(a.breachBonus?` <b>Breach +${a.breachBonus}.</b>`:'')+(a.counterBonus?` <b>Counterstrike +${a.counterBonus}.</b>`:'')+(a.captureCount?` Captured <b>${a.captureCount}</b> battle square${a.captureCount>1?'s':''}.`:'')+(healed>0?` Restored <b>${healed} HP</b>.`:'')+(fortified>0?` Gained <b>${fortified} Armor</b>.`:'')+(absorbed?` Opponent Armor blocked <b>${absorbed}</b>.`:'');
    showMoveResult(attacker,a,appliedDamage,hpDamage,absorbed);
    showPass(attacker,appliedDamage,`<b>${a.word}</b> generated ${appliedDamage} attack damage${absorbed?` (${hpDamage} reached HP)`:''}.${overlayEffects}`,true);
    }finally{moveBusy=false;renderRack();}
  }

  function swapCostForSelection(){const n=selectedRack.size;if(n===1)return 2;if(n>=2&&n<=3)return 5;if(n===7)return 10;return null;}
  function updateSwapButtons(){const n=selectedRack.size;$('swap1').disabled=reforgedThisTurn||n!==1;$('swap3').disabled=reforgedThisTurn||!(n>=2&&n<=3);$('swapAll').disabled=n!==7;}
  function toggleSwap(){ if(isCpuTurn()||cpuThinking||!isLocalTurn())return; if(placements.length){setStatus('Recall placed tiles before reforging.','bad');return;} const opening=!$('swapPanel').classList.contains('show'); $('swapPanel').classList.toggle('show'); selectedRack.clear(); renderRack(); updateSwapButtons(); setStatus(opening?(reforgedThisTurn?'Reforge already used this turn. Emergency Rerack is still available.':'Reforge mode: spend HP to repair your rack and keep your turn.'):'Reforge mode closed.','info'); }
  function performSwap(mode){
    const automatedCpuSwap=isCpuTurn();
    if(gameOver||(!automatedCpuSwap&&!remoteActionProcessing&&((!isCpuTurn()&&cpuThinking)||!isLocalTurn())))return; let inds=[...selectedRack].sort((a,b)=>a-b),cost=null;
    const emergency=mode==='all';
    if(!emergency&&reforgedThisTurn){setStatus('You already used your Reforge this turn.','bad');return;}
    if(mode==='one'&&inds.length===1)cost=2; else if(mode==='three'&&inds.length>=2&&inds.length<=3)cost=5; else if(mode==='all'&&inds.length===7)cost=10; else {setStatus('Select the correct number of letters for that option.','bad');return;}
    if(players[turn].hp<=cost){setStatus(`You need more than ${cost} HP to pay this cost.`,`bad`);return;}
    if(gameMode==='online'&&onlineRole==='guest'&&!remoteActionProcessing){
      sendGuestAction('reforge',{mode,indices:inds});
      selectedRack.clear();$('swapPanel').classList.remove('show');renderRack();return;
    }
    const old=inds.map(i=>players[turn].rack[i]); const replacements=draw(inds.length); if(replacements.length<inds.length){bag.push(...replacements);setStatus('Not enough letters remain in the bag.','bad');return;}
    if(emergency)pauseClock();
    inds.forEach((idx,k)=>players[turn].rack[idx]=replacements[k]); bag.unshift(...old); shuffle(bag);
    players[turn].hp-=cost; stats.swaps++; const who=turn; const whoLabel=playerLabel(who);
    selectedRack.clear();$('swapPanel').classList.remove('show');
    if(emergency){
      passStreak=0;stats.turns++;lastMovePlayer=who;lastMoveCells=[];addLog(`<b>${whoLabel}</b> spent <b>${cost} HP</b> on an Emergency Rerack and ended the turn.`);renderAll();showPass(who,0,`${whoLabel} spent ${cost} HP on an Emergency Rerack.`,false);return;
    }
    reforgedThisTurn=true;addLog(`<b>${whoLabel}</b> spent <b>${cost} HP</b> to Reforge ${inds.length} letter${inds.length>1?'s':''} and kept the turn.`);renderAll();setStatus(`Reforge complete: -${cost} HP. Your clock is still running — make your attack.`,'ok');sfx('heal');if(gameMode==='online')syncOnlineState();
    if(isCpuTurn())setTimeout(()=>cpuTakeTurn(),40);
  }
  function passTurn(){
    if(gameOver||(!remoteActionProcessing&&(isCpuTurn()||cpuThinking||!isLocalTurn())))return;
    if(placements.length){setStatus('Recall your placed tiles before passing.','bad');return;}
    if(gameMode==='online'&&onlineRole==='guest'&&!remoteActionProcessing){sendGuestAction('pass');return;}
    const who=turn; pauseClock(); lastMovePlayer=who;lastMoveCells=[];passStreak++; stats.turns++; addLog(`<b>${playerLabel(who)}</b> passed. <span style="color:#8f9aaa">(${passStreak}/4 stall passes)</span>`); renderStats();
    if(passStreak>=4){
      gameOver=true;
      const p1=players[0].hp,p2=players[1].hp;
      if(p1===p2) showStalemate(); else showGameOver(p1>p2?0:1,{word:'',damage:0,stalemate:true});
      return;
    }
    showPass(who,0,`${playerLabel(who)} passed without attacking.`,false);
  }

  function endTurn(){turn=1-turn;const limit=TIMER_PRESETS[timerPreset];clocks[turn]=limit;reforgedThisTurn=false;selectedRack.clear();placements=[];$('swapPanel').classList.remove('show');renderAll();if(checkSiegeAtTurnStart())return false;setStatus(isCpuTurn()?'Computer is considering its attack…':(gameMode==='online'?(isLocalTurn()?'Your turn: build an attack.':`${playerLabel(turn)} is taking their turn…`):(gameMode==='cpu'?'Your turn: build an attack.':`${playerLabel(turn)}: build an attack.`)), 'info');return true;}
  function showPass(who,damage,text,showDamage){
    if(gameMode==='online'){pauseClock();toast(showDamage?`${playerLabel(who)} hit for ${damage}`:`${playerLabel(who)} ended the turn`);if(showDamage)sfx('attack');if(!gameOver){endTurn();startClock();syncOnlineState();}else syncOnlineState();return;}
    if(gameMode==='cpu'){
      turnTransition=true;const epoch=matchEpoch;renderRack();
      $('passOverlay').classList.remove('show');
      if(who===0){
        toast(showDamage?`You dealt ${damage} damage`:'Turn ended');
        setStatus(showDamage?`Your attack landed for ${damage}. See the result above — computer responds next.`:'Turn ended — computer responds next.','ok');
        turnTransitionTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(gameOver||epoch!==matchEpoch||gameMode!=='cpu')return;turnTransition=false;if(endTurn()){startClock();cpuTakeTurn();}},3000);
      }else{
        toast(showDamage?`Computer dealt ${damage} damage`:'Computer ended its turn');
        turnTransitionTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(gameOver||epoch!==matchEpoch||gameMode!=='cpu')return;turnTransition=false;if(endTurn()){startClock();setStatus('Your turn — build an attack.','info');}},850);
      }
      return;
    }
    $('overlayTitle').textContent='Pass Device'; $('overlayText').innerHTML=`${text}<br><br>Hand the device to ${playerLabel(1-who)}. Their rack is hidden until they begin.`;
    $('overlayDamage').style.display=showDamage?'block':'none';$('overlayDamage').textContent=showDamage?`${damage} DAMAGE`:'';$('beginTurnBtn').style.display='block';$('beginTurnBtn').textContent=`Begin ${playerLabel(1-who)} Turn`; $('passOverlay').classList.add('show');
  }
  function showGameOver(winner,a,fromRemote=false){
    pauseClock(); recordMatch(winner); renderStats();
    const winnerName=playerLabel(winner);$('overlayTitle').textContent=`${winnerName} Wins`;
    const reason=a.surrender?`<b>${playerLabel(1-winner)}</b> surrendered the match.`:a.timeoutPenalty?`<b>${playerLabel(1-winner)}</b> timed out at critical health; the ${TIMEOUT_HP_COST} HP clock-burn penalty caused the knockout.`:a.siege?`<b>${winnerName}</b> held ${SIEGE_TARGET}+ battle squares with a lead of ${SIEGE_MARGIN}+ through the opponent's full turn.`:a.stalemate?`Four straight passes ended the match. ${winnerName} had more HP.`:(a.word?`<b>${a.word}</b> delivered the final ${a.damage} damage.`:'The opponent ran out of HP.');
    $('overlayText').innerHTML=`${reason}<br><br><b>Match:</b> ${stats.turns} turns • ${stats.totalDamage} total damage • biggest hit ${stats.biggestHit} • ${stats.combos} Crossfire • ${stats.counters} Counterstrike${stats.counters===1?'':'s'} • ${stats.captures} capture${stats.captures===1?'':'s'} • ${stats.timeouts} timeout${stats.timeouts===1?'':'s'}.`;
    $('overlayDamage').style.display='block';$('overlayDamage').textContent='VICTORY';$('beginTurnBtn').textContent=gameMode==='online'?'Rematch':'Play Again';$('overlaySecondaryBtn').style.display='block';$('passOverlay').classList.add('show');sfx('win');if(gameMode==='online'&&!fromRemote){syncOnlineState({winner,data:a});if(onlineRole==='host')void submitOnlineAccountResult(winner===0?'host':'guest');}
  }
  function showStalemate(fromRemote=false){
    pauseClock(); recordMatch(-1); renderStats();
    $('overlayTitle').textContent='Draw';
    $('overlayText').innerHTML=`Four straight passes ended the match with both players on ${players[0].hp} HP.<br><br><b>Match:</b> ${stats.turns} turns • ${stats.totalDamage} total damage • biggest hit ${stats.biggestHit} • ${stats.combos} Crossfire • ${stats.counters} Counterstrike${stats.counters===1?'':'s'} • ${stats.captures} capture${stats.captures===1?'':'s'} • ${stats.timeouts} timeout${stats.timeouts===1?'':'s'}.`;
    $('overlayDamage').style.display='block';$('overlayDamage').textContent='STALEMATE';$('beginTurnBtn').textContent=gameMode==='online'?'Rematch':'Play Again';$('overlaySecondaryBtn').style.display='block';$('passOverlay').classList.add('show');if(gameMode==='online'&&!fromRemote){syncOnlineState({draw:true});if(onlineRole==='host')void submitOnlineAccountResult('draw');}
  }
  function overlayAction(){
    if(gameOver&&gameMode==='online'){if(pendingIncomingRematch){pendingIncomingRematch=false;if(onlineRole==='host'){newGame(true);syncOnlineState();$('passOverlay').classList.remove('show');}else{onlineConn?.send({type:'rematchAccept'});waitingRematch=true;$('beginTurnBtn').textContent='Waiting for host…';$('beginTurnBtn').disabled=true;}return;}if(!waitingRematch){onlineConn?.send({type:'rematchRequest'});waitingRematch=true;$('beginTurnBtn').textContent='Rematch requested…';$('beginTurnBtn').disabled=true;}return;}
    $('passOverlay').classList.remove('show'); $('beginTurnBtn').style.display='block';$('beginTurnBtn').disabled=false;$('overlaySecondaryBtn').style.display='none';
    if(gameOver){newGame();return;}
    if(!matchStarted){matchStarted=true;renderAll();startClock();if(isCpuTurn())cpuTakeTurn();return;}
    if(endTurn()){startClock();if(isCpuTurn())cpuTakeTurn();}
  }
  function initCpuLexicon(){
    if(cpuLexiconReady)return;
    for(const raw of ENGLISH_WORDS){
      if(!/^[a-z]{2,11}$/.test(raw))continue;
      const w=raw.toUpperCase(); if(isContentBlocked(raw))continue; if(w.length===2&&!CPU_COMMON_2.has(w))continue; if(CPU_PROPER_EXCLUDE.has(w))continue;
      CPU_BY_LEN[w.length].push(w);
      for(let i=0;i<w.length;i++){
        const bucket=CPU_POS_INDEX[w.length][i]; (bucket[w[i]]||(bucket[w[i]]=[])).push(w);
      }
    }
    cpuLexiconReady=true;
  }
  function cpuWordKnown(w){const x=String(w).toLowerCase();return !isContentBlocked(x)&&(ENGLISH_WORDS.has(x)||rememberedWords.has(x));}
  function cpuWordAllowed(w){const x=String(w).toUpperCase();if((x.length===2&&!CPU_COMMON_2.has(x))||CPU_PROPER_EXCLUDE.has(x))return false;if(cpuDifficulty==='easy'&&/[QJXZ]/.test(x))return false;return true;}
  function rackCounts(rack){const m=Object.create(null);for(const l of rack)m[l]=(m[l]||0)+1;return m;}
  function canBuildFromRack(word,rack){const m=rackCounts(rack);for(const ch of word){if(!m[ch])return false;m[ch]--;}return true;}
  function mapWordToSlot(word,r,c,dir){
    const rack=players[turn].rack,used=new Set(),out=[];
    for(let i=0;i<word.length;i++){
      const rr=r+(dir==='V'?i:0),cc=c+(dir==='H'?i:0),fixed=board[rr][cc];
      if(fixed){if(fixed.letter!==word[i])return null;continue;}
      let ri=-1;for(let j=0;j<rack.length;j++)if(!used.has(j)&&rack[j]===word[i]){ri=j;break;}
      if(ri<0)return null;used.add(ri);out.push({r:rr,c:cc,letter:word[i],rackIndex:ri});
    }
    return out.length?out:null;
  }
  function cpuMoveScore(a,placedCount){
    const dealt=a.damage;
    const hp=players[1].hp,enemyEffective=players[0].hp+(players[0].armor||0),actualHeal=Math.min(MAX_HP,hp+a.heal)-hp,actualFortify=Math.min(MAX_ARMOR,(players[1].armor||0)+a.fortify)-(players[1].armor||0);
    let score=dealt*10 + a.comboBonus*2.5 + a.captureCount*8 + a.claimCount*2 + a.counterBonus*3 + a.breachBonus*2 + actualHeal*(hp<=35?9:4) + actualFortify*(hp<=45?5:3) + placedCount*1.2;
    if(dealt>=enemyEffective)score+=10000;
    if(hp<=25&&actualHeal)score+=actualHeal*8;
    if(a.multiplier===2)score+=7;
    if(cpuDifficulty==='easy'){
      score=dealt*1.6+actualHeal*1.5+actualFortify+a.captureCount*2+a.counterBonus*.5+Math.random()*55-Math.max(0,dealt-14)*28;
    }else if(cpuDifficulty==='normal'){
      // Normal is deliberately imperfect: it values solid damage but does not calculate like Hard.
      score=dealt*4.8+a.comboBonus*1.4+a.captureCount*3.2+a.counterBonus*1.5+actualHeal*(hp<=35?5:2)+actualFortify*1.25+Math.random()*22;
      if(placedCount===5)score-=18;
      if(placedCount===6)score-=42;
      if(placedCount>=7)score-=78;
      if(dealt>=enemyEffective)score+=1200;
    }else score+=Math.random()*1.5;
    return score;
  }
  function considerCpuPlacement(candidate,best,pool=null){
    const old=placements;placements=candidate;
    const a=analyzeMove();
    if(a.ok&&a.allWords.every(w=>cpuWordKnown(w)&&cpuWordAllowed(w))){
      const score=cpuMoveScore(a,candidate.length);
      const item={score,placements:candidate.map(x=>({...x})),analysis:a};
      if(pool){pool.push(item);if(pool.length>90){pool.sort((x,y)=>y.score-x.score);pool.length=60;}}
      if(!best||score>best.score)best=item;
    }
    placements=old;return best;
  }
  function chooseNormalCpuMove(pool,best){
    if(cpuDifficulty!=='normal'||!pool||!pool.length)return best;
    pool.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
    const top=pool.slice(0,Math.min(14,pool.length));
    const roll=Math.random();
    let lo=0,hi=Math.min(3,top.length);
    if(roll>=0.39&&roll<0.86){lo=Math.min(2,top.length-1);hi=Math.min(8,top.length);}
    else if(roll>=0.86){lo=Math.min(5,top.length-1);hi=top.length;}
    if(hi<=lo)return top[0]||best;
    return top[lo+Math.floor(Math.random()*(hi-lo))]||top[0]||best;
  }
  function findCpuMove(){
    initCpuLexicon(); const rack=players[1].rack; let best=null; const normalPool=cpuDifficulty==='normal'?[]:null; const hasBoard=board.some(row=>row.some(Boolean));
    const normalRoll=Math.random();
    const normalLengthCap=normalRoll<0.03?7:(normalRoll<0.15?6:(normalRoll<0.58?5:4));
    if(!hasBoard){
      const openingMax=cpuDifficulty==='easy'?4:(cpuDifficulty==='normal'?normalLengthCap:7);
      for(let L=2;L<=Math.min(openingMax,rack.length);L++){
        for(const w of CPU_BY_LEN[L]){
          if(!canBuildFromRack(w,rack))continue;
          for(let k=0;k<L;k++){
            let c=CENTER-k;if(c>=0&&c+L<=SIZE){const cand=mapWordToSlot(w,CENTER,c,'H');if(cand)best=considerCpuPlacement(cand,best,normalPool);}
            let r=CENTER-k;if(r>=0&&r+L<=SIZE){const cand=mapWordToSlot(w,r,CENTER,'V');if(cand)best=considerCpuPlacement(cand,best,normalPool);}
          }
        }
      }
      return chooseNormalCpuMove(normalPool,best);
    }
    for(const dir of ['H','V']){
      const maxLen=cpuDifficulty==='easy'?4:(cpuDifficulty==='normal'?normalLengthCap:11);
      for(let L=2;L<=maxLen;L++){
        const maxR=dir==='V'?SIZE-L:SIZE-1,maxC=dir==='H'?SIZE-L:SIZE-1;
        for(let r=0;r<=maxR;r++)for(let c=0;c<=maxC;c++){
          const br=r-(dir==='V'?1:0),bc=c-(dir==='H'?1:0),ar=r+(dir==='V'?L:0),ac=c+(dir==='H'?L:0);
          if(br>=0&&bc>=0&&board[br]?.[bc])continue;if(ar<SIZE&&ac<SIZE&&board[ar]?.[ac])continue;
          let fixed=[],empty=0,perpTouch=false;
          for(let i=0;i<L;i++){
            const rr=r+(dir==='V'?i:0),cc=c+(dir==='H'?i:0);
            if(board[rr][cc])fixed.push([i,board[rr][cc].letter]);
            else {empty++; if(dir==='H'){if((rr>0&&board[rr-1][cc])||(rr<SIZE-1&&board[rr+1][cc]))perpTouch=true;}else{if((cc>0&&board[rr][cc-1])||(cc<SIZE-1&&board[rr][cc+1]))perpTouch=true;}}
          }
          if(empty<1||empty>rack.length)continue;if(!fixed.length&&!perpTouch)continue;
          let words;
          if(fixed.length){
            let choice=null;
            for(const [pos,ch] of fixed){const arr=CPU_POS_INDEX[L][pos][ch]||[];if(!choice||arr.length<choice.length)choice=arr;}
            words=choice||[];
          }else{
            words=CPU_BY_LEN[L];
          }
          for(const w of words){
            let matches=true;for(const [pos,ch] of fixed)if(w[pos]!==ch){matches=false;break;}if(!matches)continue;
            const cand=mapWordToSlot(w,r,c,dir);if(!cand)continue;
            best=considerCpuPlacement(cand,best,normalPool);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return chooseNormalCpuMove(normalPool,best);
  }
  function cpuSwapOrPass(){
    const rack=players[1].rack; if(bag.length&&players[1].hp>12&&!reforgedThisTurn){
      const vowels=new Set(['A','E','I','O','U']); const v=rack.filter(x=>vowels.has(x)).length;
      const weights={Q:10,J:7,Z:7,X:6,V:4,K:4,W:3,Y:2,B:2,C:2,F:2,G:1,P:1,M:1};
      const ranked=rack.map((l,i)=>({i,l,s:weights[l]||0}));
      if(v===0)ranked.forEach(x=>{if(!vowels.has(x.l))x.s+=3;}); if(v>=5)ranked.forEach(x=>{if(vowels.has(x.l))x.s+=3;});
      if(rack.includes('Q')&&!rack.includes('U'))ranked.find(x=>x.l==='Q').s+=8;
      ranked.sort((a,b)=>b.s-a.s); let n=ranked[0]?.s>=8?1:ranked.filter(x=>x.s>=4).length>=2?Math.min(3,ranked.filter(x=>x.s>=4).length):0;
      if(n&&bag.length>=n){selectedRack=new Set(ranked.slice(0,n).map(x=>x.i)); if(n===1)performSwap('one'); else performSwap('three'); return;}
    }
    cpuPassTurn();
  }
  function cpuPassTurn(){
    if(gameOver)return; const who=turn; pauseClock(); passStreak++; stats.turns++; addLog(`<b>Computer</b> passed. <span style="color:#8f9aaa">(${passStreak}/4 stall passes)</span>`); renderStats();
    if(passStreak>=4){gameOver=true;const p1=players[0].hp,p2=players[1].hp;if(p1===p2)showStalemate();else showGameOver(p1>p2?0:1,{word:'',damage:0,stalemate:true});return;}
    showPass(who,0,'Computer could not find a worthwhile legal attack and passed.',false);
  }
  async function cpuTakeTurn(){
    if(gameOver||!matchStarted||!isCpuTurn()||cpuThinking)return; const epoch=matchEpoch;cpuThinking=true; renderAll(); setStatus(`Computer (${cpuDifficulty}) is choosing an attack…`,'info');
    await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,cpuDifficulty==='normal'?700:300));
    if(epoch!==matchEpoch||!matchStarted||!isCpuTurn())return;
    let move=null;try{move=findCpuMove();}catch(e){console.error('CPU move error',e);} syncClock();
    if(gameOver){cpuThinking=false;return;}
    if(move){
      placements=move.placements; cpuThinking=false; renderAll(); setStatus(`Computer chose ${move.analysis.word}.`,'info'); await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,cpuDifficulty==='normal'?820:460));if(epoch!==matchEpoch||!matchStarted||!isCpuTurn())return; await submitMove(false);
    }else{cpuThinking=false;renderAll();cpuSwapOrPass();}
  }

  function updatePreview(){
    const a=analyzeMove();
    const attackBtn=$('submitBtn');
    if(!a.ok){
      attackBtn?.classList.remove('readyAttack');
      $('wordPreview').textContent=placements.length?'Incomplete move':'Place a word';$('wordPreview').classList.add('empty');$('damagePreview').textContent='0';$('bonusPreview').textContent='Damage = letter power + length + visible tactical bonuses';$('wordsPreview').textContent='';$('tacticalChips').innerHTML='<span>Build a word to preview the battle result.</span>';return;
    }
    attackBtn?.classList.add('readyAttack');
    $('wordPreview').textContent=a.word;$('wordPreview').classList.remove('empty');$('damagePreview').textContent=a.damage;
    const tacticalDamage=a.comboBonus+a.controlBonus+a.breachBonus+a.counterBonus;
    const defender=1-turn,armorBlock=Math.min(players[defender]?.armor||0,a.damage),hpDamage=a.damage-armorBlock;
    const effects=[]; if(a.multiplier===2)effects.push('×2 letter hit'); if(a.comboBonus>0)effects.push(`Crossfire +${a.comboBonus}`); if(a.controlBonus>0)effects.push(`control +${a.controlBonus}`); if(a.breachBonus>0)effects.push(`Breach +${a.breachBonus}`); if(a.counterBonus>0)effects.push(`Counterstrike +${a.counterBonus}`); if(a.captureCount>0)effects.push(`capture ${a.captureCount}`); if(a.claimCount>0)effects.push(`claim ${a.claimCount}`); if(a.heal>0)effects.push(`+${a.heal} HP`); if(a.fortify>0)effects.push(`+${a.fortify} Armor`);
    let math=`Letters ${a.base}${a.multiplier===2?' ×2':''}`;if(a.bonus)math+=` + length ${a.bonus}`;if(tacticalDamage)math+=` + tactics ${tacticalDamage}`;math+=` = ${a.damage} damage`;if(armorBlock)math+=` • Armor blocks ${armorBlock} → ${hpDamage} HP`;
    $('bonusPreview').textContent=math+(effects.length?' • '+effects.filter(x=>!x.startsWith('×2 letter hit')&&!x.startsWith('Crossfire')&&!x.startsWith('control')&&!x.startsWith('Breach')&&!x.startsWith('Counterstrike')).join(' • '):'');
    const counts=controlCounts(),futureControl=counts[turn]+a.claimCount+a.captureCount;const chip=[];chip.push(`<span class="hit">${a.damage} DAMAGE</span>`);if(armorBlock)chip.push(`<span>${armorBlock} BLOCKED • ${hpDamage} HP</span>`);if(a.heal)chip.push(`<span class="heal">+${a.heal} HP</span>`);if(a.fortify)chip.push(`<span class="armor">+${a.fortify} ARMOR</span>`);if(a.captureCount)chip.push(`<span>${a.captureCount} CAPTURE${a.captureCount>1?'S':''}</span>`);if(a.comboBonus)chip.push(`<span>CROSSFIRE +${a.comboBonus}</span>`);if(a.counterBonus)chip.push(`<span>COUNTER +${a.counterBonus}</span>`);chip.push(`<span class="siege">CONTROL ${Math.min(futureControl,SIEGE_TARGET)}/${SIEGE_TARGET}</span>`);$('tacticalChips').innerHTML=chip.join('');
    $('wordsPreview').innerHTML=a.allWords.length>1?`Crossfire words: <b>${a.allWords.slice(1).join(', ')}</b> (+${a.comboBonus})`:'';
  }
  function setStatus(msg,type){statusEl.textContent=msg;statusEl.className='status '+type;}
